BUTTERNUT CREEK NEAR JAMESVILLE NY, USGS site 04245200 (USGS-04245200), stream in Onondaga County, New York, draining 32.2 square miles. Daily means from 1958-07-01 to 1999-09-30, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
